| [.agents](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/.agents ".agents") | [.agents](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/.agents ".agents") | [fix(ci): recognize current merge-gate jobs (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/2a88d7c21ad29043c7396f0547e4470e21d5235e "fix(ci): recognize current merge-gate jobs (#10627)  ## Outcome  PR description edits no longer make the maintainer checker reject successful code CI as `changes: SKIPPED`. The checker recognizes the current metadata-only workflow while retaining rejection of unknown or incomplete job evidence.  ## Reason  The current PR workflow defines 14 jobs. The checker still expects 11 names: it omits four current jobs and includes the retired `wechat-runtime-audit` job. This mismatch reproduced during review of #12339.  ## Changes  - Match the current workflow by adding `compile-artifacts`, `hugging-face-models`, `openshell-sdk-package`, and `real-openclaw-dist-harness`, and removing `wechat-runtime-audit`. - Update the synthetic workflow fixture and cover metadata edits before and after a successful code run, plus rejection of an unknown job. - Preserve existing repository, PR, head/base commit, branch, workflow, event, run, attempt, job, and status validation. - Include current main so the branch consumes the already-merged onboarding lock repair in #10465. The net PR remains three files: the checker job list and its tests.  ## Verification  - 125 focused tests passed across `check-gates-actions-evidence`, `check-gates-final-snapshot`, `check-gates-compliance`, `triage-runtime`, and `onboard-session-cross-process-lock`. - Replayed the 14-job shape from actual metadata run [36175699914](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36175699914) through the checker with synthetic, authenticated run identities. Current main rejected valid code evidence as `changes: SKIPPED`; the candidate accepted it. Both rejected an added unknown job. The replay used fake GitHub responses and a temporary home without host credential environment variables. - Confirmed the candidate's 14 names match both current `.github/workflows/pr.yaml` and that actual metadata run, with no missing or retired names. - `git diff --check` passed. The diff contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials.  ## Review notes  This changes the protected maintainer checker at `.agents/skills/nemoclaw-maintainer-day/scripts/check-gates.ts`. Manual review of the five-line production diff and its tests found no weakened identity or required-check control. Nine-category security review found no blocker. The candidate checker is exercised as the subject of isolated tests; it is not its own approval authority. Approval validation uses the unchanged checker from canonical main plus direct review of this correction.  This internal correction changes no end-user runtime or sandbox behavior, so no live E2E or public documentation change is needed. Fresh CI and scheduled review results are collected on this PR's published commit.  --- Signed-off-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ## Summary by CodeRabbit  * **Bug Fixes** * Updated CI handling for pull request metadata edits so results remain passing when edits occur before or after a successful workflow run. * CI now flags edited workflows that contain an unrecognized skipped job, helping surface incomplete or unexpected check results.  <!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ---------  Signed-off-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Prekshi Vyas <prekshiv@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Signed-off-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: github-actions[bot] <41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <prekshiv@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <34834085+prekshivyas@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com>") [#10627](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10627) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/2a88d7c21ad29043c7396f0547e4470e21d5235e "fix(ci): recognize current merge-gate jobs (#10627)  ## Outcome  PR description edits no longer make the maintainer checker reject successful code CI as `changes: SKIPPED`. | [.claude](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/.claude ".claude") | [.claude](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/.claude ".claude") | [docs(skills): make copy of docs-as-skills for Claude (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/de4fcc5010aaf335e06e20c73eb05e8509756c75 "docs(skills): make copy of docs-as-skills for Claude (#837)  * docs: docs-to-skills for claude  * docs: remove wrong target skills and rerun the conversion  * docs: use symlink  * fix: run pre-commit  * docs: pre-commit  ---------  Co-authored-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com>") [#837](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/837) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/de4fcc5010aaf335e06e20c73eb05e8509756c75 "docs(skills): make copy of docs-as-skills for Claude (#837)  * docs: docs-to-skills for claude  * docs: remove wrong target skills and rerun the conversion  * docs: use symlink  * fix: run pre-commit  * docs: pre-commit  ---------  Co-authored-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com>") | 6 months agoMar 24, 2026 |

| [.github](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/.github ".github") | [.github](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/.github ".github") | [fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/a652cfa16d4d650c5f5f6c4683deac7a7b04a09b "fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (#12197)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  OpenClaw device approval no longer hangs after a durable approval, and uncertain output delivery exits nonzero instead of reporting false success. The runtime patch now validates its complete installed shape, including the explicit-admin scope-upgrade behavior.  The OpenClaw 2026.9.1 CLI bootstrap still settles its bounded non-admin scopes automatically. A same-device local CLI upgrade to `operator.admin` remains pending for explicit approval. The managed-image activation gate now captures the exact request ID from that failed agent turn, approves only that request through `nemoclaw <sandbox> connect`, retries the normal agent turn, and still requires `PONG`.  ## Reason  PR #12178 fixed the reported approval hang, but follow-up E2E and automated review exposed several adjacent defects: direct approval retained handles, delayed exit regressed onboarding, stalled or failed stream callbacks could hide uncertain delivery, marker-only validation could accept damaged patches, the operational snapshot read a retired JSON identity, malformed pairing records could look settled, and OpenClaw silently approved the later CLI admin upgrade.  Exact-head managed-image run `35722871242` then showed that the repaired authorization boundary invalidated the general activation gate's old assumption. Both Docker and rootless Podman reached OpenClaw and correctly stopped at `scope upgrade pending approval`; direct managed startup and cleanup passed. This was a candidate-caused test-contract failure, not a runner or platform flake.  ### Related issues  Refs #12064  Follow-up to #12178.  ## Changes  - Validate exactly one approval-exit marker and the complete expected handler before accepting an installed patch. - Exit `0` only after both output callbacks confirm delivery; exit `1` on callback error, synchronous write failure, or the referenced one-second timeout. - Migrate the previously shipped unbounded and prior scope-upgrade patches, reject unknown or structurally changed shapes, and prove reapplication is byte-for-byte idempotent. - Keep bounded non-admin CLI bootstrap settlement automatic while routing the closed same-device `operator.admin` upgrade through explicit approval. - Read canonical OpenClaw SQLite pairing state through the reviewed adapter, reject malformed records, and keep sensitive values out of diagnostics. - Make the managed activation gate prove the pending-admin outcome, bind approval to the single UUID emitted by that failed turn, approve it through the existing operator connect boundary, and retry the user-facing agent command. - Preserve the existing focused issue #4462 approval flow while teaching its strict selector to use canonical SQLite identity state.  ## Verification  - Focused OpenClaw patch tests passed on `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`: 15 tests across the current-layout and migration suites. - Focused E2E-support tests passed: 27 tests across the approval fixture, canonical snapshot, and managed activation diagnostics suites. - `npm run test:changed` passed: the 7-test growth-guardrail lane and 27 selected E2E-support tests passed. - `npm run e2e:assertions:check` passed without increasing the live E2E assertion budget. - `npm run validate:pr`, commit hooks, and pre-push validation passed, including repository checks, source-shape budgets, publication validation, builds, and CLI TypeScript checks. - GitHub marks `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b` as Verified. - The 11-file diff from `main` contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials. - Focused E2E run `35686303357` on prior commit `e9028a90d745a9a32ff5bf457498d31f6697a052` proved delayed exit regressed direct approval; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35708085195` on `b1a526bac125f7a98dfb5015db935459de7a36be` exposed the stale JSON-only identity probe; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35718399558` on `a7372929b2a2cc3ebcd0fb39a85ce91f638c3cef` exposed silent CLI admin elevation; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Managed-image run `35722871242` on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` published the exact candidate, passed direct startup, and failed both exact activation jobs at the newly required explicit-admin boundary with complete cleanup.  ## Review notes  Sensitive paths: `scripts/patch-openclaw-device-self-approval.mts` and the OpenClaw managed activation approval gate.  CodeRabbit's exact-shape, observable-outcome, and idempotence findings on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` are addressed in `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`. Exact-head source CI run `35729243783` and managed-image publication run `35729243593` are pending. CodeRabbit, all PR Review Advisor specialists, and the focused manual E2E target `issue-4462-scope-upgrade-approval` must be clear for this unchanged commit before the draft is ready.  --- Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai --> ## Summary by CodeRabbit  - **Bug Fixes** - Improved device approval handling for output-drain failures, stalled output, and premature exits. - Preserved safe, repeatable approval-flow updates and rejected incomplete or structurally invalid updates. - Expanded eligible administrator-scope approvals while retaining safeguards for other requests. - Strengthened pairing-state validation so malformed or incomplete records fail clearly. - Added clearer administrator-approval failure diagnostics while protecting sensitive data from output.  - **Tests** - Expanded end-to-end coverage for approvals, pairing state, request validation, and secret redaction. <!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ---------  Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <rsliter@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <prekshiv@nvidia.com>") [#12197](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/12197) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/a652cfa16d4d650c5f5f6c4683deac7a7b04a09b "fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (#12197)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  OpenClaw device approval no longer hangs after a durable approval, and uncertain output delivery exits nonzero instead of reporting false success. The runtime patch now validates its complete installed shape, including the explicit-admin scope-upgrade behavior.  The OpenClaw 2026.9.1 CLI bootstrap still settles its bounded non-admin scopes automatically. A same-device local CLI upgrade to `operator.admin` remains pending for explicit approval. The managed-image activation gate now captures the exact request ID from that failed agent turn, approves only that request through `nemoclaw <sandbox> connect`, retries the normal agent turn, and still requires `PONG`.  ## Reason  PR #12178 fixed the reported approval hang, but follow-up E2E and automated review exposed several adjacent defects: direct approval retained handles, delayed exit regressed onboarding, stalled or failed stream callbacks could hide uncertain delivery, marker-only validation could accept damaged patches, the operational snapshot read a retired JSON identity, malformed pairing records could look settled, and OpenClaw silently approved the later CLI admin upgrade.  Exact-head managed-image run `35722871242` then showed that the repaired authorization boundary invalidated the general activation gate's old assumption. Both Docker and rootless Podman reached OpenClaw and correctly stopped at `scope upgrade pending approval`; direct managed startup and cleanup passed. This was a candidate-caused test-contract failure, not a runner or platform flake.  ### Related issues  Refs #12064  Follow-up to #12178.  ## Changes  - Validate exactly one approval-exit marker and the complete expected handler before accepting an installed patch. - Exit `0` only after both output callbacks confirm delivery; exit `1` on callback error, synchronous write failure, or the referenced one-second timeout. - Migrate the previously shipped unbounded and prior scope-upgrade patches, reject unknown or structurally changed shapes, and prove reapplication is byte-for-byte idempotent. - Keep bounded non-admin CLI bootstrap settlement automatic while routing the closed same-device `operator.admin` upgrade through explicit approval. - Read canonical OpenClaw SQLite pairing state through the reviewed adapter, reject malformed records, and keep sensitive values out of diagnostics. - Make the managed activation gate prove the pending-admin outcome, bind approval to the single UUID emitted by that failed turn, approve it through the existing operator connect boundary, and retry the user-facing agent command. - Preserve the existing focused issue #4462 approval flow while teaching its strict selector to use canonical SQLite identity state.  ## Verification  - Focused OpenClaw patch tests passed on `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`: 15 tests across the current-layout and migration suites. - Focused E2E-support tests passed: 27 tests across the approval fixture, canonical snapshot, and managed activation diagnostics suites. - `npm run test:changed` passed: the 7-test growth-guardrail lane and 27 selected E2E-support tests passed. - `npm run e2e:assertions:check` passed without increasing the live E2E assertion budget. - `npm run validate:pr`, commit hooks, and pre-push validation passed, including repository checks, source-shape budgets, publication validation, builds, and CLI TypeScript checks. - GitHub marks `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b` as Verified. - The 11-file diff from `main` contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials. - Focused E2E run `35686303357` on prior commit `e9028a90d745a9a32ff5bf457498d31f6697a052` proved delayed exit regressed direct approval; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35708085195` on `b1a526bac125f7a98dfb5015db935459de7a36be` exposed the stale JSON-only identity probe; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35718399558` on `a7372929b2a2cc3ebcd0fb39a85ce91f638c3cef` exposed silent CLI admin elevation; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Managed-image run `35722871242` on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` published the exact candidate, passed direct startup, and failed both exact activation jobs at the newly required explicit-admin boundary with complete cleanup.  ## Review notes  Sensitive paths: `scripts/patch-openclaw-device-self-approval.mts` and the OpenClaw managed activation approval gate.  CodeRabbit's exact-shape, observable-outcome, and idempotence findings on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` are addressed in `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`. Exact-head source CI run `35729243783` and managed-image publication run `35729243593` are pending. CodeRabbit, all PR Review Advisor specialists, and the focused manual E2E target `issue-4462-scope-upgrade-approval` must be clear for this unchanged commit before the draft is ready.  --- Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai --> ## Summary by CodeRabbit  - **Bug Fixes** - Improved device approval handling for output-drain failures, stalled output, and premature exits. - Preserved safe, repeatable approval-flow updates and rejected incomplete or structurally invalid updates. - Expanded eligible administrator-scope approvals while retaining safeguards for other requests. - Strengthened pairing-state validation so malformed or incomplete records fail clearly. - Added clearer administrator-approval failure diagnostics while protecting sensitive data from output.  - **Tests** - Expanded end-to-end coverage for approvals, pairing state, request validation, and secret redaction. <!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ---------  Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <rsliter@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <prekshiv@nvidia.com>") | 6 hours agoSep 26, 2026 |

| [bin](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/bin "bin") | [bin](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/bin "bin") | [chore(format): enforce Oxfmt across maintained source files (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/ef99be1e30cb78345ca5cc488a175a7bcb003f6b "chore(format): enforce Oxfmt across maintained source files (#11403)  ## Outcome  Oxfmt checks every maintained JavaScript and TypeScript file, including edits to existing files. Formatting no longer depends on Git history or a custom wrapper.  ## Reason  The previous added-file policy allowed formatting drift in existing source. Native Oxfmt file selection makes local commands and commit hooks consistent.  ## Changes  - Replace the added-file wrapper with native Oxfmt commands in npm scripts and the commit hook. - Apply the formatter across maintained source while retaining generated-output exclusions. - Add an explicit module marker to the E2E import shim so Oxfmt preserves top-level `await`; remove its lint exception. - Replace the adapter-only formatter fixtures with coverage for legacy source, scripts, plugin files, dot-directories, maintained `build` directories, and generated exclusions. - Let the E2E parity check ignore optional trailing commas and leading type separators while retaining semantic changes; verify the formatted bootstrap module through real Vitest collection. - Give the conditional-growth scan the same 60-second test allowance as the adjacent loop-growth scan for bulk diffs under coverage; keep every assertion and code budget.  ## Verification  - `npx vitest run --project integration test/automation/lint/source-formatting.test.ts` — 9 passed, including hook execution without a comparison ref. - CLI and plugin production/test type-checks — passed. - Optional-separator parity regressions — 28 passed; formatted bootstrap collection — passed. - Final diff parity — passed. Formatting, parity, and growth-guardrail suites — 82 passed. A previous focused run under coverage also passed all 82 assertions but exited 1 because whole-repository thresholds include unselected files. - AST comparison — 620 modified files have equivalent normalized syntax trees. Seven deliberate differences cover the module marker, its removed lint exception, watch-trigger mapping, parity checker and regression cases, bootstrap collection, and guardrail timeout. No changed source has parser errors. - Repository-wide formatting and lint checks — passed. - Reconciled commit: `npm run validate:pr`, `npm run lint`, CLI/plugin builds, and `npm run test:coverage:plugin` — passed. Plugin line coverage is 96.08%. - Managed startup bundle regeneration with the reviewed esbuild options — byte-identical to the committed artifact (`sha256:17ac7309b4f830947e0fcf88999c2e7b7e95cd67f880c3f6fccfac0aca2aeb6b`). - Initial `npm run check` at `400c981cb226a36536fa77c25d94a7c2f6d2fcdc` — all-file hooks and plugin coverage passed; CLI coverage finished with 34,704 passed, 46 failed, and 83 skipped tests. - The diff contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials.  Across the stack, 36 failure names also reproduce on the recorded base `e94c06f130e1904229c9214f9d1f914b98f6b1ba`. All other full-run failure cases passed a focused coverage comparison of the initial stack head `180d5280719c96e3310b2de74983f86f56bfbba1` and that base: each had 247 passed, five identical installer failures, and one skipped case. The full runs used concurrent isolated containers; the local broad gate remains red.  Checks ran in disposable checkouts using Node 22.23.1, Oxlint 1.78.0, Oxfmt 0.63.0, and an 8 GiB Node heap. The container had no network or contributor credentials. Lint configuration and hook changes were tested as candidate code; validator byte identity with canonical main is not claimed.  ## Review notes  Stack 2/3; depends on [#11402](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/11402); base: `codex/oxc-config-coverage`. Review the incremental diff against that branch. Self-review covered NVIDIA/NemoClaw commit `e6fde13d37f00be88ff416756b5f78733046bcc3`. Sensitive changes include workflow guidance, tooling, runtime scripts, sandbox code, and credentials/inference/messaging sources. AST comparison bounds the mechanical changes; independent sensitive-path review remains pending. [CodeQL alert 3154](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/security/code-scanning/3154) flags consecutive file assertions in a private test fixture. Those assertions are unchanged from the base; no authorization decision depends on the metadata check. The alert was not dismissed. This PR is a draft.  --- Signed-off-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com>  ---------  Signed-off-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: github-actions[bot] <41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com>") [#11403](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/11403) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/ef99be1e30cb78345ca5cc488a175a7bcb003f6b "chore(format): enforce Oxfmt across maintained source files (#11403)  ## Outcome  Oxfmt checks every maintained JavaScript and TypeScript file, including edits to existing files. | [ci](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/ci "ci") | [ci](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/ci "ci") | [fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/a652cfa16d4d650c5f5f6c4683deac7a7b04a09b "fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (#12197)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  OpenClaw device approval no longer hangs after a durable approval, and uncertain output delivery exits nonzero instead of reporting false success. The runtime patch now validates its complete installed shape, including the explicit-admin scope-upgrade behavior.  The OpenClaw 2026.9.1 CLI bootstrap still settles its bounded non-admin scopes automatically. A same-device local CLI upgrade to `operator.admin` remains pending for explicit approval. The managed-image activation gate now captures the exact request ID from that failed agent turn, approves only that request through `nemoclaw <sandbox> connect`, retries the normal agent turn, and still requires `PONG`.  ## Reason  PR #12178 fixed the reported approval hang, but follow-up E2E and automated review exposed several adjacent defects: direct approval retained handles, delayed exit regressed onboarding, stalled or failed stream callbacks could hide uncertain delivery, marker-only validation could accept damaged patches, the operational snapshot read a retired JSON identity, malformed pairing records could look settled, and OpenClaw silently approved the later CLI admin upgrade.  Exact-head managed-image run `35722871242` then showed that the repaired authorization boundary invalidated the general activation gate's old assumption. Both Docker and rootless Podman reached OpenClaw and correctly stopped at `scope upgrade pending approval`; direct managed startup and cleanup passed. This was a candidate-caused test-contract failure, not a runner or platform flake.  ### Related issues  Refs #12064  Follow-up to #12178.  ## Changes  - Validate exactly one approval-exit marker and the complete expected handler before accepting an installed patch. - Exit `0` only after both output callbacks confirm delivery; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Managed-image run `35722871242` on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` published the exact candidate, passed direct startup, and failed both exact activation jobs at the newly required explicit-admin boundary with complete cleanup.  ## Review notes  Sensitive paths: `scripts/patch-openclaw-device-self-approval.mts` and the OpenClaw managed activation approval gate.  CodeRabbit's exact-shape, observable-outcome, and idempotence findings on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` are addressed in `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`. Exact-head source CI run `35729243783` and managed-image publication run `35729243593` are pending. exit `1` on callback error, synchronous write failure, or the referenced one-second timeout. - Migrate the previously shipped unbounded and prior scope-upgrade patches, reject unknown or structurally changed shapes, and prove reapplication is byte-for-byte idempotent. - Keep bounded non-admin CLI bootstrap settlement automatic while routing the closed same-device `operator.admin` upgrade through explicit approval. - Read canonical OpenClaw SQLite pairing state through the reviewed adapter, reject malformed records, and keep sensitive values out of diagnostics. - Make the managed activation gate prove the pending-admin outcome, bind approval to the single UUID emitted by that failed turn, approve it through the existing operator connect boundary, and retry the user-facing agent command. - Preserve the existing focused issue #4462 approval flow while teaching its strict selector to use canonical SQLite identity state.  ## Verification  - Focused OpenClaw patch tests passed on `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`: 15 tests across the current-layout and migration suites. - Focused E2E-support tests passed: 27 tests across the approval fixture, canonical snapshot, and managed activation diagnostics suites. - `npm run test:changed` passed: the 7-test growth-guardrail lane and 27 selected E2E-support tests passed. - `npm run e2e:assertions:check` passed without increasing the live E2E assertion budget. - `npm run validate:pr`, commit hooks, and pre-push validation passed, including repository checks, source-shape budgets, publication validation, builds, and CLI TypeScript checks. - GitHub marks `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b` as Verified. - The 11-file diff from `main` contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials. - Focused E2E run `35686303357` on prior commit `e9028a90d745a9a32ff5bf457498d31f6697a052` proved delayed exit regressed direct approval; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35708085195` on `b1a526bac125f7a98dfb5015db935459de7a36be` exposed the stale JSON-only identity probe; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35718399558` on `a7372929b2a2cc3ebcd0fb39a85ce91f638c3cef` exposed silent CLI admin elevation; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Managed-image run `35722871242` on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` published the exact candidate, passed direct startup, and failed both exact activation jobs at the newly required explicit-admin boundary with complete cleanup.  ## Review notes  Sensitive paths: `scripts/patch-openclaw-device-self-approval.mts` and the OpenClaw managed activation approval gate.  CodeRabbit's exact-shape, observable-outcome, and idempotence findings on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` are addressed in `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`. Exact-head source CI run `35729243783` and managed-image publication run `35729243593` are pending. | [fern](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/fern "fern") | [fern](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/fern "fern") | [chore(format): enforce Oxfmt across maintained source files (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/ef99be1e30cb78345ca5cc488a175a7bcb003f6b "chore(format): enforce Oxfmt across maintained source files (#11403)  ## Outcome  Oxfmt checks every maintained JavaScript and TypeScript file, including edits to existing files. Formatting no longer depends on Git history or a custom wrapper.  ## Reason  The previous added-file policy allowed formatting drift in existing source. Native Oxfmt file selection makes local commands and commit hooks consistent.  ## Changes  - Replace the added-file wrapper with native Oxfmt commands in npm scripts and the commit hook. - Apply the formatter across maintained source while retaining generated-output exclusions. - Add an explicit module marker to the E2E import shim so Oxfmt preserves top-level `await`; remove its lint exception. - Replace the adapter-only formatter fixtures with coverage for legacy source, scripts, plugin files, dot-directories, maintained `build` directories, and generated exclusions. - Let the E2E parity check ignore optional trailing commas and leading type separators while retaining semantic changes; verify the formatted bootstrap module through real Vitest collection. - Give the conditional-growth scan the same 60-second test allowance as the adjacent loop-growth scan for bulk diffs under coverage; keep every assertion and code budget.  ## Verification  - `npx vitest run --project integration test/automation/lint/source-formatting.test.ts` — 9 passed, including hook execution without a comparison ref. - CLI and plugin production/test type-checks — passed. - Optional-separator parity regressions — 28 passed; formatted bootstrap collection — passed. - Final diff parity — passed. Formatting, parity, and growth-guardrail suites — 82 passed. A previous focused run under coverage also passed all 82 assertions but exited 1 because whole-repository thresholds include unselected files. - AST comparison — 620 modified files have equivalent normalized syntax trees. Seven deliberate differences cover the module marker, its removed lint exception, watch-trigger mapping, parity checker and regression cases, bootstrap collection, and guardrail timeout. No changed source has parser errors. - Repository-wide formatting and lint checks — passed. - Reconciled commit: `npm run validate:pr`, `npm run lint`, CLI/plugin builds, and `npm run test:coverage:plugin` — passed. Plugin line coverage is 96.08%. - Managed startup bundle regeneration with the reviewed esbuild options — byte-identical to the committed artifact (`sha256:17ac7309b4f830947e0fcf88999c2e7b7e95cd67f880c3f6fccfac0aca2aeb6b`). - Initial `npm run check` at `400c981cb226a36536fa77c25d94a7c2f6d2fcdc` — all-file hooks and plugin coverage passed; CLI coverage finished with 34,704 passed, 46 failed, and 83 skipped tests. - The diff contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials.  Across the stack, 36 failure names also reproduce on the recorded base `e94c06f130e1904229c9214f9d1f914b98f6b1ba`. All other full-run failure cases passed a focused coverage comparison of the initial stack head `180d5280719c96e3310b2de74983f86f56bfbba1` and that base: each had 247 passed, five identical installer failures, and one skipped case. The full runs used concurrent isolated containers; the local broad gate remains red.  Checks ran in disposable checkouts using Node 22.23.1, Oxlint 1.78.0, Oxfmt 0.63.0, and an 8 GiB Node heap. The container had no network or contributor credentials. Lint configuration and hook changes were tested as candidate code; validator byte identity with canonical main is not claimed.  ## Review notes  Stack 2/3; depends on [#11402](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/11402); base: `codex/oxc-config-coverage`. Review the incremental diff against that branch. Self-review covered NVIDIA/NemoClaw commit `e6fde13d37f00be88ff416756b5f78733046bcc3`. Sensitive changes include workflow guidance, tooling, runtime scripts, sandbox code, and credentials/inference/messaging sources. AST comparison bounds the mechanical changes; independent sensitive-path review remains pending. [CodeQL alert 3154](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/security/code-scanning/3154) flags consecutive file assertions in a private test fixture. Those assertions are unchanged from the base; no authorization decision depends on the metadata check. The alert was not dismissed. This PR is a draft.  --- Signed-off-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com>  ---------  Signed-off-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: github-actions[bot] <41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com>") [#11403](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/11403) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/ef99be1e30cb78345ca5cc488a175a7bcb003f6b "chore(format): enforce Oxfmt across maintained source files (#11403)  ## Outcome  Oxfmt checks every maintained JavaScript and TypeScript file, including edits to existing files. | [managed-inference](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/managed-inference "managed-inference") | [managed-inference](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/managed-inference "managed-inference") | [chore(deps): update OpenClaw to 2026.9.1 (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/85290d3248abdb56b077debee6fb08b18b921ea8 "chore(deps): update OpenClaw to 2026.9.1 (#11105)  ## Outcome  Updates the managed OpenClaw runtime from 2026.7.1 to 2026.9.1. The change carries the new release through the image build, runtime compatibility patches, reviewed dependency and lifecycle policy, optional-plugin integrity pins, tests, and Pi qualification evidence.  ## Why  NemoClaw needs an exact, reviewed 2026.9.1 candidate so current-head CI and the full same-repository E2E workflow can prove the release works across the supported managed-runtime paths.  ## Scope  - Pins the OpenClaw 2026.9.1 package and lock graphs. - Updates the reviewed npm audit and lifecycle records. - Adapts the managed image and compatibility patches to the 2026.9.1 package layout and runtime behavior. - Keeps optional plugin downloads independently integrity-pinned. - Updates focused unit, integration, package-contract, and E2E coverage. - Records the reviewed Pi candidate qualification receipts for both amd64 and arm64.  This PR is limited to the OpenClaw release migration. It does not implement unrelated device-pair `publicUrl` or tunnel configuration work.  ## Verification  - Reviewed runtime bundle regeneration check: passed. - OpenClaw managed-image contract test: 19/19 passed. - Focused OpenClaw and trust/audit suites: 254 passed, 1 intentionally skipped. - Real OpenClaw 2026.9.1 tarball harness: 6/6 passed, including SQLite token rotation and rollback. - Signed pre-commit, publication validation, typecheck, formatting, lint, shellcheck, security, and growth gates: passed locally. - Exact-head CI: running on `c8348b769cf965530e48ad2d49f6383060616e07`. - CodeRabbit: exact-head review pending. - PR Review Advisor: pending exact-head CI gate. - Full same-repository PR E2E: pending exact-head CI and review cleanup.  ## Review notes  Documentation review: `no-docs-needed`. This migration changes managed image, runtime compatibility, dependency policy, and validation internals without adding a user-facing configuration surface. | [nemoclaw-blueprint](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/nemoclaw-blueprint "nemoclaw-blueprint") | [nemoclaw-blueprint](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/nemoclaw-blueprint "nemoclaw-blueprint") | [fix(policy): use read-only raw GitHub defaults in Balanced (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/96b8354b33aced28db1a5c691572ef3028f3b6d1 "fix(policy): use read-only raw GitHub defaults in Balanced (#10487)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  Fresh Balanced onboarding permits only GET and HEAD to `raw.githubusercontent.com` through its Homebrew preset. Open keeps unrestricted raw GitHub methods, and Personal retains its broad web policy. OpenShell owns enforcement; NemoClaw adds no proxy filtering.  Existing Homebrew grants are preserved when Balanced defaults are reselected. An operator must explicitly remove `brew` to narrow that grant. This is the accepted maintainer decision.  Fixes #10380.  ## Changes  - Add `brew-balanced` and select it only in Balanced. All other Homebrew endpoints and binary scopes match `brew`. - Preserve the existing onboarding selection implementation. Test that suggested defaults retain existing Homebrew and custom grants in interactive and unattended onboarding. Keep explicit removal and saved selections authoritative. - Register the new policy file in the Hermes portable build allowlist and include genuine Pi qualification receipts for the shared image change. - Extend the existing Deep Agents egress check to require raw GitHub GET/HEAD success and structured local POST denial. Use curl to exercise the Homebrew grant from the original report. - Repair the existing Deep Agents lifecycle fixture's Podman container lookup, which used a Docker-specific ownership label. Keep exact sandbox-name and before/after container identity checks. - Document the new default and explicit grant-removal procedure.  ## Validation  Final revision: `5fdc1ad9cd6bbf942b0fea4e5441e1190e65add0`.  - [Required CI](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36148513962) passed, including all 12 CLI test groups. - [Managed-image qualification](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36148513961) passed, including Pi AMD64/ARM64 and Docker/rootless Podman activation. One Docker image-download transport failure passed on a job-only retry. - The [focused PR run](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36152651286) passed policy-selection sequencing on Docker and Podman. Both Deep Agents scenarios passed lifecycle checks, all cloud checks, and cleanup. On both runtimes, the retained probe output proves raw GitHub GET/HEAD success and local POST denial with `policy_denied` and no upstream HTML. - The complete focused workflow remains failed because of subsequent configuration-export checks. Podman export is explicitly unsupported on the base and candidate. Docker's export command and semantic comparisons passed, but its pinned Rust consumer failed; the runner truncated the final cause. A controlled local comparison reproduced an existing incompatibility: both base and candidate policies use `strict` Landlock, while the pinned consumer accepts the older `hard_requirement` spelling. Both fixtures pass when only that spelling is adjusted for diagnosis. Production policy and export behavior were not changed. These export limitations are outside this policy fix; no complete E2E pass is claimed. - 177 focused policy, onboarding, lifecycle, and runtime-provider tests passed. Four grant-preservation cases failed before the repair. The existing pinned-v1 defaults compatibility tests and Podman-deferral test also passed locally. - Documentation validation passed with zero errors and two existing warnings. Generated guide variants contain the grant-preservation rule.  ## Review and qualification  CodeRabbit completed a full review with no actionable findings, and all review threads are resolved. All nine Advisor specialists completed. Eight were clear; the inherited test-file-only selection gap is nonblocking because the complete PR diff selects the Deep Agents target. The [maintainer disposition](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10487#issuecomment-5834663663) records the planner evidence.  The maintainer rejected automatic replacement of existing grants. That logic is removed, addressing CodeRabbit's concern about historical manual grants. The existing synchronizer is unchanged. The explicit recovery instructions now identify the actual policy-list markers.  The one-time [qualification bootstrap](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10487#issuecomment-5826541601) is exhausted. Genuine AMD64/ARM64 Pi receipts come from [run 36094005129](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36094005129), source `baf09152b38a30491a5da045e61f1134daaabe1c`. The final repair does not change image inputs. All subsequent commits and pushes use normal validation.  --- Signed-off-by: Yimo Jiang <yimoj@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com>  ---------  Signed-off-by: Yimo Jiang <yimoj@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: github-actions[bot] <41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <34834085+prekshivyas@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com>") [#10487](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10487) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/96b8354b33aced28db1a5c691572ef3028f3b6d1 "fix(policy): use read-only raw GitHub defaults in Balanced (#10487)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  Fresh Balanced onboarding permits only GET and HEAD to `raw.githubusercontent.com` through its Homebrew preset. Open keeps unrestricted raw GitHub methods, and Personal retains its broad web policy. OpenShell owns enforcement; NemoClaw adds no proxy filtering.  Existing Homebrew grants are preserved when Balanced defaults are reselected. An operator must explicitly remove `brew` to narrow that grant. This is the accepted maintainer decision.  Fixes #10380.  ## Changes  - Add `brew-balanced` and select it only in Balanced. All other Homebrew endpoints and binary scopes match `brew`. - Preserve the existing onboarding selection implementation. Test that suggested defaults retain existing Homebrew and custom grants in interactive and unattended onboarding. Keep explicit removal and saved selections authoritative. - Register the new policy file in the Hermes portable build allowlist and include genuine Pi qualification receipts for the shared image change. - Extend the existing Deep Agents egress check to require raw GitHub GET/HEAD success and structured local POST denial. Use curl to exercise the Homebrew grant from the original report. - Repair the existing Deep Agents lifecycle fixture's Podman container lookup, which used a Docker-specific ownership label. Keep exact sandbox-name and before/after container identity checks. - Document the new default and explicit grant-removal procedure.  ## Validation  Final revision: `5fdc1ad9cd6bbf942b0fea4e5441e1190e65add0`.  - [Required CI](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36148513962) passed, including all 12 CLI test groups. - [Managed-image qualification](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36148513961) passed, including Pi AMD64/ARM64 and Docker/rootless Podman activation. One Docker image-download transport failure passed on a job-only retry. - The [focused PR run](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36152651286) passed policy-selection sequencing on Docker and Podman. Both Deep Agents scenarios passed lifecycle checks, all cloud checks, and cleanup. On both runtimes, the retained probe output proves raw GitHub GET/HEAD success and local POST denial with `policy_denied` and no upstream HTML. - The complete focused workflow remains failed because of subsequent configuration-export checks. Podman export is explicitly unsupported on the base and candidate. Docker's export command and semantic comparisons passed, but its pinned Rust consumer failed; the runner truncated the final cause. A controlled local comparison reproduced an existing incompatibility: both base and candidate policies use `strict` Landlock, while the pinned consumer accepts the older `hard_requirement` spelling. Both fixtures pass when only that spelling is adjusted for diagnosis. Production policy and export behavior were not changed. These export limitations are outside this policy fix; no complete E2E pass is claimed. - 177 focused policy, onboarding, lifecycle, and runtime-provider tests passed. Four grant-preservation cases failed before the repair. The existing pinned-v1 defaults compatibility tests and Podman-deferral test also passed locally. - Documentation validation passed with zero errors and two existing warnings. Generated guide variants contain the grant-preservation rule.  ## Review and qualification  CodeRabbit completed a full review with no actionable findings, and all review threads are resolved. All nine Advisor specialists completed. Eight were clear; the inherited test-file-only selection gap is nonblocking because the complete PR diff selects the Deep Agents target. The [maintainer disposition](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10487#issuecomment-5834663663) records the planner evidence.  The maintainer rejected automatic replacement of existing grants. That logic is removed, addressing CodeRabbit's concern about historical manual grants. The existing synchronizer is unchanged. The explicit recovery instructions now identify the actual policy-list markers.  The one-time [qualification bootstrap](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10487#issuecomment-5826541601) is exhausted. Genuine AMD64/ARM64 Pi receipts come from [run 36094005129](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/36094005129), source `baf09152b38a30491a5da045e61f1134daaabe1c`. The final repair does not change image inputs. All subsequent commits and pushes use normal validation.  --- Signed-off-by: Yimo Jiang <yimoj@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com>  ---------  Signed-off-by: Yimo Jiang <yimoj@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: github-actions[bot] <41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Apurv Kumaria <akumaria@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Carlos Villela <cvillela@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <34834085+prekshivyas@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Aaron Erickson <aerickson@nvidia.com>") | 16 hours agoSep 25, 2026 |

| [schemas](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/schemas "schemas") | [schemas](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/schemas "schemas") | [fix(config): export the verified selected Ollama model (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/4e5ccf7225b60e2aeab84eff194b2686cfbd2baf "fix(config): export the verified selected Ollama model (#11891)  ## Outcome  `nemoclaw config export` now exports the sandbox’s selected, installed Ollama model and verified SHA-256 digest. A selection such as `qwen2.5:0.5b` succeeds when all observations agree; `qwen3.5:9b` retains its existing output.  ## Reason  The observer and public schema fixed the served name to `qwen3.5:9b`, even when the retained selection, live route, daemon and authenticated proxy agreed on another installed model.  ### Related issues  Fixes #11857. Refs #11432, #11433, #11434, #11435 and #10904.  ## Changes  - Use the existing bounded model-name contract in the observer and schema, and regenerate the public JSON schema. - Preserve independent daemon/proxy digest checks, selected-entry uniqueness, route and retained-selection agreement, stable snapshots, credential custody and publication behavior. - Extend behavioral coverage for non-default selections, invalid/duplicate/missing models, conflicting digests, route drift and changing proxy evidence. - Qualify the non-default model through the existing `gpu-e2e` export scenario. Its selected-name assertion replaces a redundant provider-field assertion already covered by the complete adapter tests and strict schema; the E2E assertion budget is unchanged.  Scope decision: **Accept**, recording the requester’s explicit selected-model implementation scope and approval in the implementation session. Accountable maintainer: San Dang (@sandl99). The reason is to export the model already in use, through the existing observation → verification → document builder → schema → publication pipeline.  The compatibility boundary is native Linux, Docker, OpenClaw, an externally managed local Ollama daemon and the existing authenticated proxy. Existing default-model output remains valid. Daemon lifecycle changes, custom builds, additional platforms and unrelated installed models remain excluded. Export stays read-only, with credential references only. Validation consists of failing behavioral regressions, focused pipeline/schema tests, a real proxy integration test, regenerated schema checks and the existing installed-CLI `gpu-e2e` scenario.  The live fixture preinstalls the selected model before onboarding and prepares the attached daemon before export. All model preparation occurs outside export. The command reference now describes the selected model and digest.  ## Verification  - Before the fix: the new observer and complete-export regressions failed; 44 existing tests passed. - Focused schema, observer, action, snapshot, verifier, document-builder and GPU-support suites passed. After correcting test setup, `node_modules/.bin/vitest run --project cli --project integration src/lib/adapters/config/live-export-source-ollama.test.ts test/automation/pull-requests/growth-guardrails.test.ts` passed all 32 cases. - `npm run config-schema:check`, `npm run e2e:assertions:check`, CLI/plugin builds, and `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=8192 npm run typecheck:cli` passed. - `node_modules/.bin/vitest run --project package-contract test/package-contract/config-schema.test.ts` passed. - The real proxy integration test passed in isolated Linux with external networking disabled. It observed exactly two `GET /api/tags` backend requests, unchanged retained proxy files, and no credential disclosure. - Changed-file lint, formatting and `git diff --check` passed. The diff contains no secrets, API keys or credentials. - Normal commit hooks passed, including schema, repository, semantic-phase, source-shape, growth, secret-scan and commit-message checks. - Normal pre-push publication validation and CLI typecheck passed. GitHub verified the signed commit. - All 43 GPU helper tests passed, including selected-model precedence over the workflow default. `node --import tsx scripts/checks/e2e-mock-parity.mts --base origin/main --head HEAD` passed. - All 24 Ollama capability tests passed. Fixture preinstallation uses the default daemon listener before onboarding switches to the export port; no production lifecycle code changes. - `npm run docs` passed with zero errors and two warnings. Generated command-reference variants retain the stated OpenClaw-only Ollama scope. - All 33 PR CI checks passed on `639d4d9ec2c0769177f4bf70fdf7ac664da2fbaa`. The [installed-CLI GPU qualification](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35081368180) passed all three scenarios, including selected-model export, repeated-spec agreement and stopped-daemon refusal. Immutable dispatch/result artifacts match this commit. All scenario cleanup receipts and Docker authentication cleanup passed.  ## Review notes  Self-review covers NVIDIA/NemoClaw commit `639d4d9ec2c0769177f4bf70fdf7ac664da2fbaa`, including the sensitive paths `src/lib/inference/ollama/proxy-observation.ts` and its test. The change preserves the existing read-only observation and credential boundary. The complete nine-specialist Advisor review of the preceding commit found no production correctness or security issue. Its two findings concern the scope record, now stated above from the requester’s instruction, and the command-reference wording, corrected in this repair. Previous review evidence does not establish approval of the repair commit. The PR remains a draft. CI and live qualification passed on this commit. All nine specialists completed [Advisor run35082687798](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35082687798); seven recorded no blockers. The gate remains failed on these two fixture findings, which are disputed by the following evidence. No waiver or merge approval is claimed.  - `F-operability-recovery-e0c4c584f6276e66c8ee`: cleanup is already registered [before preparation](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/blob/639d4d9ec2c0769177f4bf70fdf7ac664da2fbaa/test/e2e/live/gpu-e2e.test.ts#L440). The [fixture teardown](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/blob/639d4d9ec2c0769177f4bf70fdf7ac664da2fbaa/test/e2e/fixtures/e2e-test.ts#L238) runs registered cleanup in `finally`. All 15 existing cleanup-resource tests passed, as did the live cleanup receipts. - `F-delivery-flow-29d732c294e8e2151a69`: the attached daemon reported `{\"models\":[]}` before its pull, despite successful preparation through the installed service. This is retained in `export-daemon-ready-attempt-03.result.json` inside [qualification artifact10440414828](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35081368180/artifacts/10440414828). The attached pull is required to populate that daemon. Its result is retained, and the subsequent export/name/digest assertions prevent an ineffective preparation from passing. Removing it would break the qualified fixture. A more localized pull-status assertion is a diagnostic suggestion, not an established P1 failure.  The current commit stays unchanged pending maintainer disposition of those findings. The approved `gpu-e2e` selector passed; broader generic review-queue E2E selectors were not run.  --- Signed-off-by: San Dang <sdang@nvidia.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ## Summary by CodeRabbit  - **New Features** - Ollama exports now support selecting different served models instead of requiring a single fixed model. - Exported configurations record and verify the selected model name and SHA-256 digest.   - Model names are validated for safe length and character content.   - Exported model selections override default workflow model settings.  - **Bug Fixes** - Improved validation detects mismatches or instability across proxy, daemon, startup, and retained model information.  <!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ---------  Signed-off-by: San Dang <sdang@nvidia.com>") [#11891](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/11891) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/4e5ccf7225b60e2aeab84eff194b2686cfbd2baf "fix(config): export the verified selected Ollama model (#11891)  ## Outcome  `nemoclaw config export` now exports the sandbox’s selected, installed Ollama model and verified SHA-256 digest. | [scripts](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/scripts "scripts") | [scripts](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/scripts "scripts") | [fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/a652cfa16d4d650c5f5f6c4683deac7a7b04a09b "fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (#12197)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  OpenClaw device approval no longer hangs after a durable approval, and uncertain output delivery exits nonzero instead of reporting false success. The runtime patch now validates its complete installed shape, including the explicit-admin scope-upgrade behavior.  The OpenClaw 2026.9.1 CLI bootstrap still settles its bounded non-admin scopes automatically. A same-device local CLI upgrade to `operator.admin` remains pending for explicit approval. The managed-image activation gate now captures the exact request ID from that failed agent turn, approves only that request through `nemoclaw <sandbox> connect`, retries the normal agent turn, and still requires `PONG`.  ## Reason  PR #12178 fixed the reported approval hang, but follow-up E2E and automated review exposed several adjacent defects: direct approval retained handles, delayed exit regressed onboarding, stalled or failed stream callbacks could hide uncertain delivery, marker-only validation could accept damaged patches, the operational snapshot read a retired JSON identity, malformed pairing records could look settled, and OpenClaw silently approved the later CLI admin upgrade.  Exact-head managed-image run `35722871242` then showed that the repaired authorization boundary invalidated the general activation gate's old assumption. Both Docker and rootless Podman reached OpenClaw and correctly stopped at `scope upgrade pending approval`; direct managed startup and cleanup passed. This was a candidate-caused test-contract failure, not a runner or platform flake.  ### Related issues  Refs #12064  Follow-up to #12178.  ## Changes  - Validate exactly one approval-exit marker and the complete expected handler before accepting an installed patch. - Exit `0` only after both output callbacks confirm delivery; exit `1` on callback error, synchronous write failure, or the referenced one-second timeout. - Migrate the previously shipped unbounded and prior scope-upgrade patches, reject unknown or structurally changed shapes, and prove reapplication is byte-for-byte idempotent. - Keep bounded non-admin CLI bootstrap settlement automatic while routing the closed same-device `operator.admin` upgrade through explicit approval. - Read canonical OpenClaw SQLite pairing state through the reviewed adapter, reject malformed records, and keep sensitive values out of diagnostics. - Make the managed activation gate prove the pending-admin outcome, bind approval to the single UUID emitted by that failed turn, approve it through the existing operator connect boundary, and retry the user-facing agent command. - Preserve the existing focused issue #4462 approval flow while teaching its strict selector to use canonical SQLite identity state.  ## Verification  - Focused OpenClaw patch tests passed on `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`: 15 tests across the current-layout and migration suites. - Focused E2E-support tests passed: 27 tests across the approval fixture, canonical snapshot, and managed activation diagnostics suites. - `npm run test:changed` passed: the 7-test growth-guardrail lane and 27 selected E2E-support tests passed. - `npm run e2e:assertions:check` passed without increasing the live E2E assertion budget. - `npm run validate:pr`, commit hooks, and pre-push validation passed, including repository checks, source-shape budgets, publication validation, builds, and CLI TypeScript checks. - GitHub marks `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b` as Verified. - The 11-file diff from `main` contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials. - Focused E2E run `35686303357` on prior commit `e9028a90d745a9a32ff5bf457498d31f6697a052` proved delayed exit regressed direct approval; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35708085195` on `b1a526bac125f7a98dfb5015db935459de7a36be` exposed the stale JSON-only identity probe; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35718399558` on `a7372929b2a2cc3ebcd0fb39a85ce91f638c3cef` exposed silent CLI admin elevation; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Managed-image run `35722871242` on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` published the exact candidate, passed direct startup, and failed both exact activation jobs at the newly required explicit-admin boundary with complete cleanup.  ## Review notes  Sensitive paths: `scripts/patch-openclaw-device-self-approval.mts` and the OpenClaw managed activation approval gate.  CodeRabbit's exact-shape, observable-outcome, and idempotence findings on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` are addressed in `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`. Exact-head source CI run `35729243783` and managed-image publication run `35729243593` are pending. CodeRabbit, all PR Review Advisor specialists, and the focused manual E2E target `issue-4462-scope-upgrade-approval` must be clear for this unchanged commit before the draft is ready.  --- Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai --> ## Summary by CodeRabbit  - **Bug Fixes** - Improved device approval handling for output-drain failures, stalled output, and premature exits. - Preserved safe, repeatable approval-flow updates and rejected incomplete or structurally invalid updates. - Expanded eligible administrator-scope approvals while retaining safeguards for other requests. - Strengthened pairing-state validation so malformed or incomplete records fail clearly. - Added clearer administrator-approval failure diagnostics while protecting sensitive data from output.  - **Tests** - Expanded end-to-end coverage for approvals, pairing state, request validation, and secret redaction. <!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ---------  Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <rsliter@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <prekshiv@nvidia.com>") [#12197](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/12197) [)](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/a652cfa16d4d650c5f5f6c4683deac7a7b04a09b "fix(openclaw): validate approval exit patch shape (#12197)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  OpenClaw device approval no longer hangs after a durable approval, and uncertain output delivery exits nonzero instead of reporting false success. The runtime patch now validates its complete installed shape, including the explicit-admin scope-upgrade behavior.  The OpenClaw 2026.9.1 CLI bootstrap still settles its bounded non-admin scopes automatically. A same-device local CLI upgrade to `operator.admin` remains pending for explicit approval. The managed-image activation gate now captures the exact request ID from that failed agent turn, approves only that request through `nemoclaw <sandbox> connect`, retries the normal agent turn, and still requires `PONG`.  ## Reason  PR #12178 fixed the reported approval hang, but follow-up E2E and automated review exposed several adjacent defects: direct approval retained handles, delayed exit regressed onboarding, stalled or failed stream callbacks could hide uncertain delivery, marker-only validation could accept damaged patches, the operational snapshot read a retired JSON identity, malformed pairing records could look settled, and OpenClaw silently approved the later CLI admin upgrade.  Exact-head managed-image run `35722871242` then showed that the repaired authorization boundary invalidated the general activation gate's old assumption. Both Docker and rootless Podman reached OpenClaw and correctly stopped at `scope upgrade pending approval`; direct managed startup and cleanup passed. This was a candidate-caused test-contract failure, not a runner or platform flake.  ### Related issues  Refs #12064  Follow-up to #12178.  ## Changes  - Validate exactly one approval-exit marker and the complete expected handler before accepting an installed patch. - Exit `0` only after both output callbacks confirm delivery; exit `1` on callback error, synchronous write failure, or the referenced one-second timeout. - Migrate the previously shipped unbounded and prior scope-upgrade patches, reject unknown or structurally changed shapes, and prove reapplication is byte-for-byte idempotent. - Keep bounded non-admin CLI bootstrap settlement automatic while routing the closed same-device `operator.admin` upgrade through explicit approval. - Read canonical OpenClaw SQLite pairing state through the reviewed adapter, reject malformed records, and keep sensitive values out of diagnostics. - Make the managed activation gate prove the pending-admin outcome, bind approval to the single UUID emitted by that failed turn, approve it through the existing operator connect boundary, and retry the user-facing agent command. - Preserve the existing focused issue #4462 approval flow while teaching its strict selector to use canonical SQLite identity state.  ## Verification  - Focused OpenClaw patch tests passed on `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`: 15 tests across the current-layout and migration suites. - Focused E2E-support tests passed: 27 tests across the approval fixture, canonical snapshot, and managed activation diagnostics suites. - `npm run test:changed` passed: the 7-test growth-guardrail lane and 27 selected E2E-support tests passed. - `npm run e2e:assertions:check` passed without increasing the live E2E assertion budget. - `npm run validate:pr`, commit hooks, and pre-push validation passed, including repository checks, source-shape budgets, publication validation, builds, and CLI TypeScript checks. - GitHub marks `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b` as Verified. - The 11-file diff from `main` contains no secrets, API keys, or credentials. - Focused E2E run `35686303357` on prior commit `e9028a90d745a9a32ff5bf457498d31f6697a052` proved delayed exit regressed direct approval; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35708085195` on `b1a526bac125f7a98dfb5015db935459de7a36be` exposed the stale JSON-only identity probe; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Focused E2E run `35718399558` on `a7372929b2a2cc3ebcd0fb39a85ce91f638c3cef` exposed silent CLI admin elevation; bindings were exact and cleanup completed. - Managed-image run `35722871242` on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` published the exact candidate, passed direct startup, and failed both exact activation jobs at the newly required explicit-admin boundary with complete cleanup.  ## Review notes  Sensitive paths: `scripts/patch-openclaw-device-self-approval.mts` and the OpenClaw managed activation approval gate.  CodeRabbit's exact-shape, observable-outcome, and idempotence findings on `59cdf7cd44802eec83adeec6601ce7ed85480481` are addressed in `82a21f121711e26872f2a33aed2823720e95591b`. Exact-head source CI run `35729243783` and managed-image publication run `35729243593` are pending. CodeRabbit, all PR Review Advisor specialists, and the focused manual E2E target `issue-4462-scope-upgrade-approval` must be clear for this unchanged commit before the draft is ready.  --- Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai --> ## Summary by CodeRabbit  - **Bug Fixes** - Improved device approval handling for output-drain failures, stalled output, and premature exits. - Preserved safe, repeatable approval-flow updates and rejected incomplete or structurally invalid updates. - Expanded eligible administrator-scope approvals while retaining safeguards for other requests. - Strengthened pairing-state validation so malformed or incomplete records fail clearly. - Added clearer administrator-approval failure diagnostics while protecting sensitive data from output.  - **Tests** - Expanded end-to-end coverage for approvals, pairing state, request validation, and secret redaction. <!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ---------  Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Signed-off-by: Rebecca Sliter <rsliter@nvidia.com> Co-authored-by: Rebecca Sliter <571084+rsliter@users.noreply.github.com> Co-authored-by: Prekshi Vyas <prekshiv@nvidia.com>") | 6 hours agoSep 26, 2026 |
| [skills](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/skills "skills") | [skills](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/tree/main/skills "skills") | [fix(pi): close release contract gaps (](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/commit/7d3428718f2591b2023ffe2bb72ed990e6856f81 "fix(pi): close release contract gaps (#10355)  <!-- markdownlint-disable MD041 --> ## Outcome  Pi rebuild preserves recorded context-window, output-token, and reasoning settings. JSON automation excludes project context files. Pi qualification checks the context-free task after fresh onboarding, rebuild, and recovery, and verifies interactive session persistence.  Pi remains unavailable to ordinary installations pending #8818.  ## Reason  Rebuild reconstruction dropped Pi tuning. Automation examples accepted project context, and the qualification flow lacked a fresh-onboarding proof for the context-free task.  ### Related issues  Resolves #7929. Refs #7928 and the accepted Pi decision in #7926.  ## Changes  - Replay recorded Pi tuning through the existing startup-profile builder; test explicit false, upper bounds, omitted values, and conflicting ambient inputs. - Document Pi model limits, candidate-image authority, lifecycle operations, recovery, and trust boundaries. Add the model-limits route to both documentation-routing copies. - Seed hostile context files and run `pi-read-v2` with `--no-context-files`. Retain the independent read-tool oracle for fresh onboarding, rebuild, and recovery. - Preserve the PTY settling repair from #11853. Require the interactive turn to add session state and require rebuild to preserve the session inventory. - Use the canonical verified pre-onboarding cleanup owner from main; remove the obsolete best-effort helper. Force termination of the deterministic seed harness on timeout. - Isolate vLLM selection tests from real host-readiness probes. Include the merged SDK delivery dependency from #11921 for credential-free installation during qualification.  ## Verification  - Current candidate: `b205695cc6f7fa4e9a50d57ff306fd644c1dc01f`, including canonical main `052c19e9a7f65d0ea3dd2e6e6202aa27fe7ef476`. Fresh hosted CI, automated review, and Pi qualification are pending; the successful run below qualifies the earlier commit only. - Current integration checks: signed-commit hooks passed; Pi rebuild and vLLM selection suites passed 46 tests. Pi oracle and PTY support suites passed; the client suite had one unchanged five-second timeout whose two ForwardTcp cases passed in isolation.  - Repair commit: `3127f1b62897ceeeb5f938be5f9938b97abefedf`. - Pi task/oracle support suite: 22 tests passed. Published-route suite: 63 tests passed, including exact source mappings for Pi-only onboarding and commands. - Semantic phase validation, E2E assertion ratchet, repository checks, source-shape and growth checks, secret scan, and normal signed-commit hooks passed. - Publication validation, CLI/plugin builds, CLI/plugin TypeScript, and JavaScript configuration checks passed. The guarded fast-forward push and branch/PR readback match the repair commit; all PR commits are GitHub Verified. - The inspected diff and secret scan contain no secrets or credentials. - [Hosted CI](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35173912638) and the complete [managed-image workflow](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35173912251) passed, including native AMD64 and ARM64 Pi builds. - [Live AMD64 Pi qualification passed](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35177167273/job/105061800208) on `3127f1b62897ceeeb5f938be5f9938b97abefedf`. The retained [evidence artifact](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35177167273/artifacts/10479695594) contains `pi-agent-qualification.json`, `evidence-manifest.json`, all three independent `pi-read-v2` task proofs, session-persistence evidence, policy and credential-boundary checks, and successful cleanup. All seven phases passed; all three read tasks passed on their first attempts. `Relevant E2E` also passed. This is focused Pi coverage, not full-suite release qualification. - Flakiness retained: the [first run of the same candidate](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35176205131/job/105058784992) failed on an inference invocation HTTP 503 during stop/start after onboarding and rebuild passed. The [exact-base comparison](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/actions/runs/35176719363) failed earlier on the absent-gateway pre-cleanup bug this PR fixes, so that comparison is inconclusive. One bounded unchanged-candidate rerun passed. The HTTP 503 cause remains unclassified; no assertion or failure handling was weakened.  ## Review notes  The ARM64 lifecycle finding is excluded under the [accepted September 3 amendment](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/issues/7926#issuecomment-5531655141), as recorded in the [PR disposition](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10355#issuecomment-5706725693). Full lifecycle qualification runs on AMD64. ARM64 still requires native managed-image build, startup, publication, and matching receipt evidence. This does not waive CI or authorize public Pi activation. The lifecycle artifact above and both platform image jobs in the linked managed-image workflow provide the retained qualification evidence. They do not resolve the inherited review findings or authorize merge.  All nine hosted Advisor specialist reports for `0eb1437` were collected. The fresh-onboarding finding and CodeRabbit seed-timeout finding are repaired. CodeRabbit completed review of `71f352f`; its additional Pi source-mapping finding is repaired. The merge retains the current canonical receipt pair and matching authority. CodeRabbit completed review of `3127f1b` with six findings in files inherited unchanged from canonical `main` commit `9c36cd9cd72e07194f6593928a63038e47f11678` (merged #11906). Their file blobs match that canonical commit exactly, including the WeChat sibling of the synchronous-process finding. These are retained as inherited findings outside the Pi repair: [review and six findings](https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw/pull/10355#pullrequestreview-5230448942). The scope warning also comes from those merged base changes; the net Pi delta against the current canonical integration base is 17 files. Self-review of the repair preserves the independent task oracle, cleanup failure propagation, and interactive persistence checks.  <!-- nemoclaw-docs-review:start --> - Documentation review: `docs-updated` - Documentation evidence: Verified Pi model-limit bounds, rebuild tuning replay, models.json generation, nproc/nofile limits, setpriv privilege drop, corporate-CA merge, writable paths, and all documented commands and flags against checked-in source; changed documentation reviewed against WRITING.md and STYLE.md; verdict PASS with no blocking findings. - Documentation agent: independent read-only review subagent <!-- docs-review-head-sha: e4ff78361657e6052b41956a9497fbab2dff985e --> <!-- docs-review-agents-blob-sha: 43145d5a12720240f35ab52c5b4d97fcb21b7e20 --> <!-- nemoclaw-docs-review:end -->  --- Signed-off-by: Tinson Lai <tinsonl@nvidia.com> Signed-off-by: Kao Félix <me@kaofelix.dev> Signed-off-by: Julie Yaunches <jyaunches@nvidia.com>   <!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai -->  ## Summary by CodeRabbit  -
